866 entries, agentrelay to agentskills-create-plugin. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **agentrelay**: Use when supervising an overnight or unattended coding run with AgentRelay — setting up guards/validators/budget, finishing a unit safely (validate → checkpoint), reading the morning report, or when the user mentions agentrelay, supervised runs, guards, checkpoints, or "don't touch these files". Teaches the correct order of operations so work is gated on evidence and the user's own uncommitted work is never lost. ([MalyStern/agentrelay](https://github.com/MalyStern/agentrelay/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/agentrelay) · ★1)
- **agentrewardbench-eval**: This benchmark evaluates the effectiveness of LLM-based judges in automatically assessing web agent trajectories. It probes the judges' ability to correctly predict task success, detect side effects, and identify repetitive actions by comparing their outputs against expert human annotations. Use when the user wants to benchmark on AgentRewardBench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports precision. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/agentrewardbench-eval) · ★3)
- **agentring**: Reach the user on their phone through agentring — send a message, wait for their reply, or call them (a voice AI talks to them for you and returns the transcript). Use when you need the user's decision or attention and they may be away from the screen, when a long task finishes or fails, or when the user asks you to message or call them. Also use when the user asks to set up, install or connect agentring. ([mutonby/agentring](https://github.com/mutonby/agentring/tree/HEAD/daemon/src/agentring/skill) · ★454 · 3 copies)
- **agentripe**: Discover, buy, and sell AI agent services on Agentripe — the Stripe for AI Agents. Autonomous monetization via x402 protocol with on-chain identity and escrow. ([Agentripe/Agentripe](https://github.com/Agentripe/Agentripe/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agentripe) · ★1)

- **agentrs-map**: 高德地图 / 地理工具箱 —— 通过京东 agentrs 代理网关，用单一 Bearer Token 调 6 个高德(Amap)工具： POI 地点检索、地理编码(地址↔经纬度/adcode)、行政区域查询、IP 定位、步行路径规划、坐标转换。 当需要查地点经纬度与 adcode、找附近地点(POI)、做步行导航、转换坐标系、 按 IP 定位、查行政区划时使用。触发词：高德、地图、经纬度、坐标、adcode、地理编码、 POI、附近、步行路线、导航、坐标转换、IP定位、行政区、省市区。 ([hanlinlibham/skills](https://github.com/hanlinlibham/skills/tree/HEAD/agentrs-map) · ★2)

- **agentrs-weather**: 天气查询工具 —— 通过京东 agentrs 代理网关调用高德天气，返回城市实况(气温/天气/湿度/风力) 或未来预报。支持直接传高德 adcode，也支持传城市名/地址自动解析 adcode。当用户问某地天气、 气温、今天/明天下不下雨、出门带不带伞、未来几天天气这类问题时使用。 触发词：天气、气温、下雨、温度、湿度、风力、今天天气、明天天气、未来天气、weather、带伞。 ([hanlinlibham/skills](https://github.com/hanlinlibham/skills/tree/HEAD/agentrs-weather) · ★2)
